本次報告：法說會

瑞昱 (2379 TT) 
財報與展望均優於預期

永豐觀點 
目前股價持穩於前季法說時點，而公司在財報與展望(包括毛利率、營業費用控管)
似乎均有好轉現象，尤其毛利率似乎出現谷底回溫 (優於研究部原預期的內外承
壓)，又公司在網通/車載等業務持續進展，股價應有上升空間。 
 投資評價與建議 
中立調升至買進：由於財報與展望均優於預期，毛利率谷底回溫，營業費用謹慎控
管，故研究部上修獲利預估值，預估瑞昱2026 年稅後 EPS 30.64 元(原預估 27.66
元)，2027 年稅後 EPS 35.19 元(原預估 29.15 元)。研究部認為瑞昱(1) 身為晶片
大廠，在資源調度、產品完整度、晶片優化或議價能力，相對小廠更具競爭力 (或
許也有機會提高市場份額)，此似乎也體現於 3Q26 財報與 4Q26 展望中，(2) 中
長期受會網通/車載/伺服器/邊緣 AI 裝置涉足而帶動營運成長，故將評等由中立調
升至買進建議，目標價 915 元(以 26X2027 年 EPS 計算)。

ESG 評析 
瑞昱企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2025 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。
SinoPac Securities Investment Service licenses and applies the SASB Materiality Map® 
General Issue Categories and Disclosure Topics in our work.

### Page 2

2

瑞昱為網通晶片大廠 ：瑞昱為全球網通晶片大廠，若以公司營收排名為全球第八大
IC 設計廠商。瑞昱產品線含括 Ethernet、WiFi、藍芽、Switch 與 TV SoC 。網通
領域的參與廠商為博通、Marvell、高通與聯發科等，TV SoC 的參與廠商則包括聯
發科、聯詠等。 
3Q26 獲利優於預期：瑞昱 3Q26 營收 377.32 億元(+0.5%QoQ，+27.9% YoY)，
毛利率 47.4%，稅後獲利 41.26 億元(+8%QoQ，+20%YoY)，稅後 EPS 8 元，獲
利優於預期，主要是營收、毛利率與營業費用控管均較預期佳。瑞昱3Q26 財報細
項要點如下：(1) 營收季增 0.5%，優於研究部原預期的季減 3%，主要是網通業務
成長驅使，以及部分產品價格調漲，從而抵銷筆電/TV/遊戲機需求減緩的衝擊。若
究終端應用分拆，PC 因終端市場需求疲軟，瑞昱 3Q26 PC 比重由前季的 33%降
至 30%(3Q26 PC: 非 PC 比重為 30%:70%。(2) 毛利率由 2Q26 的 47%微幅增至
47.4%(優於研究部原預期將受製造成本上升而使毛利率承壓)，主要是產品結構變
化(高階產品比重增加 )，以及產品因應成本上揚而進行轉嫁。 (3) 營業費用季減
0.6%，反映公司在投資研發的同時，仍對營業費用謹慎控管。(4) 業外收益僅 6.06
億元(季減 3900 萬元)， 主要是包含金融資產評價損失 。(5) 瑞昱庫存天數雖由2Q26
的 107 天增至 3Q26 的 136 天，不過主要是供應鏈持續有不確定性，公司為確保
客戶供貨的連貫性，故多建置庫存因應。 
4Q26 營收降幅不會如同過往第四季顯著：瑞昱認為(1) 4Q26 營運審慎穩健，雖
第四季通常為營運淡季， 但隨產品結構往高階挪移， 又價格為反映製造成本上升而
仍有調漲事宜，故營收降幅較為平緩，其中PC 需求仍相對疲弱。(3) 2H26 毛利率
將逐步回溫。整體而言，研究處預估瑞昱 4Q26 營收 348.5 億元(-7.6%QoQ，
+32.6%YoY)，毛利率隨產品結構變化與轉嫁而略增至 47.6%，稅後獲利 35.34 億
元(-14%QoQ，+33%YoY)，稅後 EPS 6.86 元。

營運現況與分析

### Page 3

3

2027 年營運應仍能維持成長力道：研究部認為台系 IC 設計族群今明年將持續遭
遇內外交雜的挑戰(內有製造成本攀升、外有消費電子需求疲軟)，不過瑞昱身為晶
片大廠，在資源調度、產品優化與議價能力均相對同業更具競爭力，使得營運表現
應能優於產業狀態(其實從瑞昱財報亦能得證，公司即使 30%營收與 PC 相關，但
其 3Q26 獲利數字與 4Q26 展望均仍穩健)。研究部認為瑞昱動能來自下述因子驅
動：(1) 網通隨電信業者對 25GPON 架構進行結構性測試、乙太網路交換器受益
於硬體升級、消費者與中小企業路由器往Ｍesh/三頻配置/WiFi7 升級，並且企業
級網路管理需求與管理型交換器持續普及中，均將推升瑞昱網通業務長期穩健成
長。(2) WiFi7 滲透率提升。隨瑞昱客戶的新開案均基於 WiFi7 作設計，瑞昱預期
WiFi7 滲透率將由 2026 年的 30%攀升至 40%。WiFi 聯盟計畫 WiFi8 將於 06/2027
完成認證測試，並將於 09/2027 前正式啟動 WiFi8 認證，瑞昱預期 2H27 公司
WiFI8 產品可望步入量產，以利客戶搭載 WiFi8 產品更廣泛於 2028 年上市。(3) 
光通訊專注 112G/224G SerDes 開發(4nm 製程)，100G 光模組控制晶片將以雲
端、資料中心與電信網路為主要著墨項目，明年量產規模提升，並且 400G/800G 
PAM4 DSP預計 2H27 推出。(4) 電動車成長持續帶動瑞昱車載乙太網路進展，瑞
昱預期新車搭載乙太網路的滲透率已超過 50%。(5) 中長期邊緣 AI 業務(包括車載
/機器人/IOT)與高效能運算 (比如瑞昱導入乙太網路 /交換器/xPON/SSD/IO 橋接
器/USB hub/光通訊)，目標 2030 年營收比重達 30%。 
中立轉買進：由於財報與展望均優於預期，毛利率谷底回溫，營業費用謹慎控管，
故研究部上修獲利預估值，預估瑞昱2026 年營收 1466 億元(+19%YoY)，毛利率
47.91%，稅後獲利 157.9 億元(+7%YoY)，稅後 EPS 30.64 元(原預估 27.66 元)，
並預估 2027 年營收 1594 億元(+9%YoY)，毛利率 48.37%，稅後獲利 181.4 億
元(+15%YoY)，稅後 EPS 35.19 元(原預估 29.15 元)。研究部認為瑞昱(1) 身為晶
片大廠，在資源調度、產品完整度、晶片優化或議價能力，相對小廠更具競爭力(或
許也有機會提高市場份額)，此似乎也體現於 3Q26 財報與 4Q26 展望中，(2) 中
長期受會網通/車載/伺服器/邊緣 AI 裝置涉足而帶動營運成長，故將評等由中立調
升至買進建議，目標價 915 元(以 26X2027 年 EPS 計算)。

## Document 097: 2383台光電 合庫

Source PDF: all report pdf/2383台光電 合庫.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-04

### Page 1

TAIWAN COOPERATIVE SECURITIES 
INVESTMENT CONSULTANT

1

台光電(2383)     買進 報告日期：20261104 
                                                  研究員：陳彥翰

公司基本資料 
前日收盤價(元) 4,395.00  
目前股本(億元) 35.83  
市值(億元) 15,747.30  
每股淨值(元) 205.03  
外資持股比例(%) 42.40  
投信持股比例(%) 11.88  
董監持股比例(%) 8.58  
融資餘額(張) 3,308

個股簡評 
高階銅箔基板產能供不應求，載板可望通過
驗證並成為新成長動能，以上將帶動產品組
合持續大幅優化，推升報價和毛利率，讓今
明兩年營運獲利續創歷史新高 。合庫預估  
2026 / 2027 年獲利分別成長 205% / 121%，
稅後每股盈餘  127.17 / 280.45  元，建議買
進，目標價 5,610 元 (2027 PER 20x)。 
業務營收比重

公司簡介  
台光電子材料股份有限公司成立於 1992 年，
初期主要供應  FR-4 銅箔基板與膠片；並於  
2013 年 ， 成為全球最大無鹵素基板供應商 ， 迄
今仍在市場保持領先地位 。 台光電持續開發出
各種優良的無鹵素新產品，從  Mid. Loss 、 
Low Loss、Very Low Loss、Ultra Low Loss 到 
Extreme Low Loss 等不同等級材料 ， 並符合各
種 高 精 密 先 進 PCB 技 術 ， 如Anylayer 、
mSAP、封裝載板、超高層板、高速傳輸及高
頻產品等各應用場域之需求。

簡易損益表 
單位：百萬 2025 2026(F) 2027(F) 3Q25 4Q25 1Q26 2Q26 3Q26 4Q26(F) 1Q27(F) 2Q27(F)  
營業收入 94,261  208,630  373,576  25,146  24,927  33,067  47,275  60,885  67,403  75,635  87,301   
營業毛利 28,120  73,728  151,010  7,576  7,125  9,729  16,003  22,951  25,045  30,152  35,161

2

營運展望 
1. 3Q26 需求強勁及漲價效應帶動營收獲利續創歷史新高：高速基材需求強勁、
帶動產品組合持續優化、及全品項漲價效應顯現，第三季營收續創歷史新高，
亦同時帶動毛利率進一步上揚。台光電第三季營收 609 億元、+28.8% QoQ、
+142.1% YoY， 毛利率 37.7%。 營業利益 187 億元、+46.8% QoQ、+274.5% YoY，
營業利益率 30.7%。歸屬母公司稅後淨利 145 億元，每股盈餘 40.47 元。期
末存貨總額提升至 271 億元，細項中原物料達 178 億元 、 製成品約 70 億元 、
在製品約 22 億元及商品約 7,774 萬元 ； 增備存貨主要著眼於高階原料市場吃
緊，藉由提前拉高原料水位以保障後續生產。 
2. 4Q26 需求仍然強勁將延續前幾季成長動能 ：第四季受惠產品結構與定價優勢 ，
將延續全產全銷強勁營運態勢，預估整體營收可望季增兩成。毛利率預期也因
定價優勢可進一步明顯提升。整體成長動能涵蓋 AI 伺服器、通用伺服器、高
階交換器、低軌衛星及記憶體模組等多重領域。高階基材整體價格持續調漲，
全產品線綜合漲價幅度預期落在 5 ~ 20% 之間 。基礎設施延續漲價效應及產品
組合優化將季增兩成，行動裝置因需求呈現持平，車用與工業則因產能優先挪
往高毛利產品，營收受排擠將季減約一成。 
3. 載板近期可望通過驗證並成為新一波成長動能：硬質銅箔基板涵蓋高密度互連
材料 (High Density Interconnect, HDI)、高速材料 (High Speed Digital, HSD) 與
載板 (Substrate) 三大範疇，雖然日韓對手高階 HDI 技術與台光電相仿，但擴
產停滯且侷限本土生產；台光電憑藉靈活量產規模與成本優勢，競爭力顯著勝
出。為追求極速傳輸與超低延遲，AI 系統架構加速走向模組化設計。單板晶片
密度倍增直接帶來線寬與線距極限微縮、高層數壓合等多重嚴苛的高階製程考
驗。台光電整合 HDI、高速與載板技術，推動「HDI 載板化」 ，確保板材歷經
多次高溫壓合仍具備載板級穩定性與可靠度。 
4. 產品組合持續往高階低損耗優化，新產能長約綁定將全產全銷：高階超低損耗
材料滲透率維持上升格局，M7+ 營收占比已從去年 50 ~ 55% 提升至 65 ~ 70%，
隨著 M9 下半年步入量產 ， 明年出貨規模放大 ， 佔比將進一步墊高至 70 ~ 75%。
產能也將自目前 585 萬張擴充至 2030 年 1,525 萬張。面對高階銅箔基板供
需日益緊俏且合規供應商有限下，終端客戶近期主動要求簽署長期供貨協議。 
5. 產品組合優化報價毛利率攀升，獲利將再創歷史新高：高階銅箔基板產能供不
應求，帶動產品組合持續大幅優化，推升報價和毛利率，今明年營運獲利將續
創歷史新高。 合庫預估 2026 年營收 2,086 億元、+121.3% YoY， 毛利率 35.3%。
營業利益 591 億元、+209.4% YoY，營業利益率 28.3%。歸屬母公司稅後淨利 
457 億元，每股盈餘 127.17 元。 
6. ESG (台光電在 2026 年上市公司治理評鑑結果為 6 ~ 20%)：台光電子針對各

項製造、服務與活動所衍生之空氣污染問題能加以管制及掌控。除了基本遵行

 Update
ASE Technology Holding Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
  CPO Revenue on Track; Stay 
OW 
 CPO is on track for >US$500mn in 2027: Management mentioned the CPO has 
already started mass production  and will reach at least US$500mn in 2027. This is 
in-line with our estimates that we forecast CPO revenue to be >US$600mn in 2027.
Slightly upward revision of LEAP revenue: The company slightly raised 2026 LEAP 
(leading-edge advanced packaging) revenue from US$3.7bn to US$4bn and  2027 
from >US$7.5bn to >US$8bn, on track  to our forecast of US$9bn. It expects at least 
US$1bn of full process CoWoS-like revenue in 2027 vs. MSe of US$1.8bn.
Margin expansion is also on track: 3Q26 ATM gross margin has already surpassed 
the structural range and reached 30.6%. The company mentioned 30% would be 
the new floor and expects it to continue to expand sequentially in 2027.
3Q26 GM and 4Q26 guidance slightly beat: 3Q26 GM of 22.1% is slightly higher 
than both MSe and the consensus. 4Q26 revenue guidance of 6-8% QoQ growth 
and GM of 23-24% are both slightly higher than MSe of 5% QoQ growth and 22-23% 
GM forecast.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
ASE Technology Holding Co. Ltd. (3711.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity of 9.8%, intermediate
growth rate of 10%, terminal growth rate of 3.5%, and cash dividend payout ratio of 60%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected global economic and semiconductor growth
n Margin expansion through merger consolidation
n New wearables project wins with higher margins
Risks to Downside

n Slower-than-expected global economic and semiconductor growth
n Further margin erosion amid price competition
n Lack of new features in wearables, causing consumers to lose interest

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

3Q26 results overview 
AWSC’s 3Q26 core business OP was NT$209mn, which was lower 
than both GSe/BBG consensus of NT$226mn/251mn, mainly due to 
the ongoing impact from rising material cost, leading to a 24.3% GM 
(lower than GSe/BBG consensus GM of 25.6%/29.3%), despite UTR 
in 3Q26 remaining stable at 65-70%. 
For earnings, AWSC 3Q26 EPS was 3% higher than GSe but was 9% 
lower than BBG consensus on the contracted GM. 
Valuation 
We maintain our Neutral rating with a 12M TP of NT$147, based on 
27x 4Q26-3Q27E P/E, which is line with the company’s past 10-year 
forward P/E multiple, and in line with the inventory build-up period 
average P/E.

### Page 3

Investment Thesis and TP & risks

AWSC is one of the largest GaAS foundries globally, with 10-15% market share in the 
GaAS foundry industry in 2025. We expect the company to enjoy solid share gain 
opportunity in the long term, considering its much lower than peers’ production cost 
and better production efficiency. However, given the high inventory level in the supply 
chain, and the slower-than-expected Android smartphone PA demand in coming 
quarters, we expect relatively slow profit growth in the near term. 
We are Neutral rated given the company’s relatively slow profit growth in coming 
quarters. We think the stock is priced fairly, trading at its historical upcycle average P/E 
multiple, despite the long-term market share growth opportunity. We forecast AWSC’s 
net income to grow at a 34% 2026-28E CAGR. The key up/downside risks are (1) 
easing/rising competition in the China PA foundry market, (2) 
faster/slower-than-expected progress on 5G/WiFi 7 upgrade trends, and (3) 
faster/slower-than expected progress with new businesses, including LEO satellite, 
drones and datacom.  
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$147 is based on a target 27x 
4Q26-3Q27 P/E, which is in line with the company’s past 10-year forward P/E multiple 
and the inventory build-up period average P/E. We consider this multiple appropriate 
given the soft demand outlook and our positive view on the company’s longer-term 
share gain opportunity. 
The key up/downside risks are 1) easing/rising competition in the China PA foundry 
market, (2) faster/slower-than-expected progress on 5G/WiFi 7 upgrade trends, and (3) 
faster/slower-than expected progress with new businesses, including LEO satellite, 
drones and datacom.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：由於產能取得狀況優於預期，研究部上修獲利預估值，預估創意 2026
年稅後 EPS 57.89 元(原預估 50.33 元)，2027 年稅後 EPS 183.83 元。考量創意具
備 Google CPU 與 Tesla AI5、車載等專案挹注，帶動營運顯著成長，研究部維持
創意買進評等 ， 目標價由5570 元調升至 10,350 元(2027~2028 年 EPS 均值與 45
倍評價)。

ESG 評析 
創意企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B+等與 A 等。

### Page 2

2

創意為 IC 設計服務公司：創意成立於 01/1998 為台積電轉投資，為國內首家專業
提供 SoC 設計代工服務，並持續開發矽智財的公司。創意 3Q26 營收結構為 NRE
與 IP11%、量產晶片 89%，公司跟隨台積電腳步重心往 AI 與 HPC 領域挪移，並
開發先進封裝製程所需的矽智財與工程服務。全球矽智財公司包括 ARM、
Synopsys 等，而國內相關 IC 設計服務公司包括創意與世芯，矽智財公司則包括
力旺、円星等。 
3Q26 財報優於預期：創意 3Q26 營收 187.55 億元(+35%QoQ，+118%YoY)，
毛利率 20.6%， 營業利益率13.6%， 稅後獲利22.72 億元(+46%QoQ，+162%YoY)，
稅後 EPS 16.95 元 ， 財報優於預期 ， 主要是營收(產能取得更多使營收表現更突出)、
毛利率與營業費用控管程度優於預期。創意 3Q26 財報細項表現如下：(1) 營收季
增 35%，其中 NRE 與 IP 總計 20.8 億元(-10%QoQ，營收佔比 11%)、量產晶片
166.71 億元(+44%QoQ，營收佔比 89%)，量產晶片主要來自 Google CPU 挹注
(量產晶片 3nm 以下佔比 59%)。創意 3Q26 產品結構以雲端佔比 86%為重心
(2Q26 雲端佔比 79%)， 此主要包括CPU、AI 加速器與 BMC。(2) 毛利率由 2Q26
的 21.5%小降至 20.6%，雖因量產晶片佔比由前季的 83%增至 89%，但毛利率仍
超逾 20%水準(僅較 2Q26 下降 0.9%)。(3) 營業費用季減 1%，公司表示公司已屬
高薪水位，故整體員工分紅比例將向下調低。 
未給予 4Q26 展望 ：由於時處發行 ECB 緘默期 ， 創意並未給予4Q26 營運目標值 ，
不過研究部考量雲端 廠案件持續挹注，預估創意 4Q26 營收 196.72 億元
(+5%QoQ，+59%YoY)，毛利率 19.8%，稅後獲利 22.84 億元 (+1%QoQ，
+97%YoY)，稅後 EPS 17.04 元。 
營運顯著成長： 研究部對創意營運正向以待，預期未來營 運動能將來自於 (1) 
Google CPU 放量成長，研究部預估 Google CPU 專案今明年挹注營收 10.5 億
/38.5 億美元，相當於營收比重 51%/56%，成為營運最大宗的貢獻。(2) T esla AI5
預計將於 2H27 步入量產階段，研究部以 T esla每年出貨量約 170 萬台為基準估
算，假設 50%投片於台系龍頭晶圓廠，則將挹注創意明年營收 8.5 億美元，而該
客戶專案為 TK1(表示毛利率優於 CPU 專案)，反映公司本身具備技術優勢。(3) 車
載案件遍佈中國歐洲與北美，中國(地平線)歐洲客戶均已 tape out，將成為後續量
產晶片動能，而地平線已於 4Q26 小量出貨。研究部以地平線預估明年出貨 700
萬台為基準，假設征程 7 系列滲透率 50%，並且參考地平線低中高階晶片的終端
售價自 20~500 美元不等 ， 以此反推創意可能的單價計算 ， 則明年營收約貢獻4.55

億美元。(4) 信驊、微軟均持續挹注訂單。中期亦可觀察 Google 其餘案件是否亦
將承成為驅動力，以及長期光通訊與新創公司合作進展。 
維持買進建議：由於產能取得狀況優於預期，研究部上修獲利預估值。由於創意營
收規模擴大，公司也謹慎控管營業費用，未來應能朝向營運槓桿的方向邁步，故研
究部傾向以營業利益率來作為獲利率的呈現 。 研究部預估創意2026 年營收 637.72
億元(+87%YoY)，營業利益率 13.5%，稅後獲利 77.57 億元(+105%YoY)，稅後
EPS 57.89 元(原預估 50.33 元)，並且調整 2027 年獲利預估模型，預估 2027 年
營收 2142 億元 (+236%YoY) ， 營 業 利 益 率13.5% ， 稅 後 獲 利246 億元
(+218%YoY)，稅後 EPS 183.83 元。考量創意具備 Google CPU 與 Tesla AI5、車
載等專案挹注，帶動營運顯著成長，研究部維持創意買進評等，目標價由5570 元
調升至 10,350 元(由 40X2027~2028 年 EPS 均值，改以 45X 評價)。

주가 추이

3분기 영업 이 익 은 지상방산 매출 감소와 일 회성 비용 등의 영 향 으로 
기대치를 대폭 하회했습니다. 4분기 실적 회복 과 중 장기 실적 성장 대
비 저평가된 밸 류 에이 션 에 주목합 니 다. 
3Q26 Review: 영업이익 0.5조원으로 컨센서스 대폭 하회 
3분기 매출액은 6.1조원으로 전년대비 6.0% 감소했다. 항공우주 부문
과 연결자회사들 성장에도 불구하고 지상방산 매출이 전년대비 약 0.9
조원 감소한 영향이다. 지상방산 내수와 수출 매출은 각각 0.7조원, 0.6
조원으로 전년대비 28.6%, 50.4% 감소했다. 내수는 대전 사업장 생산 
중단, 수출은 4분기 납기 집중에 기인한다. 항공우주 매출은 군수사업 
물량 증가로 전년대비 9.3% 증가한 0.7조원을 기록했다. 연결 자회사
인 한화오션과 한화시스템 매출은 각각 3.4조원, 0.8조원으로 전년대비 
12.9%, 30.1% 증가했다. 영업이익은 0.5조원으로 전년대비 49.2% 감
소했으며 영업이익률은 7.4%로 6.3%P 하락했다. 한화오션과 한화시스
템의 이익 증가에도 지상방산 이익 감소와 필리조선소 적자 반영이 영
향을 미쳤다. 지상방산은 일회성 비용 약 460억원, 원화 강세, 매출 감
소에 따른 고정비 부담 증가가 복합적으로 작용했다. 세전이익은 한화
오션의 외화환산손실 영향으로 -0.2조원을 기록하며 적자전환했다.  
실적 회복과 저평가 매력에 주목할 시점 
4분기 영업이익은 1.7조원으로 추정하며 시장 기대치를 상회할 전망이
다. 지상방산 수출 인도 물량이 집중되는 영향이다. 2027년 예상 실적 
기준 P/E는 12.5배로 경쟁사 Rheinmetall(22.5배) 대비 낮다. 약 35조
원의 방산 수주잔고를 기반으로 최소 2029년까지 실적 성장이 예상되
는 만큼 저평가 매력이 높다. 신규 수주 시점과 실적에 대한 우려가 상
존하지만 현재 밸류에이션에는 상당 부분 반영된 것으로 판단한다. 미
국 중간선거 등 국방예산 관련 정책 불확실성과 환율 부담이 완화되면 
투자자들의 관심은 4분기 실적과 2027년 신규 수주로 이동할 전망이
다. 연말 주요국의 국방예산 확정은 신규 수주 시점에 대한 불확실성을 
낮추는 계기가 될 수 있다.  
투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 1,300,000원 유지 
글로벌 재무장 수요 확대에 따른 동사의 중장기 성장 전망은 여전히 
유효하다. 투자의견 BUY와 목표주가 1,300,000원을 유지한다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10 월 30 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (채운샘) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
한화에어로스페이스는 당사와 ‘독점규제 및 공정거래에 관한 법률’ 에 따른 계열회사 관계에 있는 법인입니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

6 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

## Document 271: AI is boosting business creations both in the US and Europe (2026-11-04)

### Page 1

4 November 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
AI IS BOOSTING BUSINESS CREATIONS BOTH IN THE US AND EUROPE
Stéphane Colliac
The significant increase in business creations since mid-2025 in the United States, France and Germany – and, to a lesser extent, in the United
Kingdom – is continuing. Across the board, this trend is being driven by the information and communication sector and mostly by AI. It is
spreading to other business services, while developments in consumer services, industry and construction are more mixed. However, in both

the United States and France, the trend is broadening, suggesting that AI is facilitating business creation across all sectors.
Business creations by sector: a boom that is building momentum and spreading

We have calculated the year-on-year change in the number of new business start-ups. The table categorises these results as positive (red), neutral (white) or negative (blue). The darker
the colour, the larger the change. The data series are defined as follows: Germany = new entries in the companies register (start-ups) since 2008; France = business start-ups, including

micro-entrepreneurs, since 2000; United Kingdom = businesses registered for VAT/PAYE since 2017; United States = business applications (tax identification number) since 2004. Sectors
have been grouped together to ensure that the classification is standardised across all countries. For the third quarter, the German data only include the months of July and August.

### Page 2

2
The bank for
a changing
world
Chart of the Week / 4 November 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
A noticeable boom in the United States, France, and Germany, but not quite as pronounced in the United Kingdom
The four economies for which we have comparable data (the United States, France, Germany and the United Kingdom) have all
seen, to varying degrees, an increase in the number of new business creations. While the timing varies across these countries,

most of them are enjoying strong momentum. In the United States, there has been a noticeable acceleration since the second
quarter of 2025, which has continued to strengthen. Germany has followed a similar trajectory, moving from slight declines at
the start of 2024 to sharp increases. France is experiencing the same trend but with some specific features, as momentum has
picked up significantly after months of slowdown (due to the uncertainty following the dissolution of the National Assembly) and is

accelerating across most sectors in 2026. In the United Kingdom, the trend is less pronounced and the increase may not last.
Business services, and AI in particular, are driving this momentum
In most of these four countries, business creations are rising significantly in the information and communication sector, which is
directly linked to AI. In the United States, Germany and France, there has been a pronounced trend which is gaining momentum.
In the United Kingdom, the AI sector is also seeing an increase, but it is significantly less pronounced. However, this momentum is

not limited to this sector. It is spreading to many services sectors, including finance, real estate and business services. Growth has
been sustained since mid-2025 in the United States and Germany, and since the third quarter of 2025 in France.
The picture for consumer services is more mixed. In the United States, trade, accommodation and catering have experienced major
increases in creations since mid-2025. In France, these sectors are in positive territory, particularly trade, where the increase in

business creations has recently accelerated. In Germany, these same sectors fell into negative territory in 2026 following a rather
mixed year in 2025.
The situation is even more mixed for manufacturing and construction. In the United States, these sectors are also part of the
overall positive momentum in the third quarter of 2026. This shows that AI does not only benefit sectors closely linked to it
(notably the digital technology sector) but that it makes it easier to start a business across the entire economy (by simplifying

administrative procedures in particular). In France, the upturn in business creations in the construction sector suggests that this
same trend is at play, even as activity in the sector continues to contract.
Therefore, the trend is more widespread in the United States and is becoming increasingly prevalent in France. It is driven by
specific sectors of the services industry in Germany, while it is significantly more subdued in the United Kingdom.

stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com
Article written with the help of Ece Usta (intern).

Key points
• Total dwelling approvals rose 5.1%mth
in September, although the annual pace
moved into contraction at –0.8%yr with
the detail suggesting the underlying
cycle is starting to turn down.

• The headline increase was driven by
another volatile rise in private unit
approvals (+16.6%) which more than
offset a significant decline in private
detached house approvals (–5.3%).

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Total dwelling approvals rose 5.1%mth in September, while
the annual pace moved into contraction at –0.8%yr. The
result was well above both Westpac’s expectation for a
7.0%mth decline and the market expectation for a 2.0%mth
rise. Unit approvals continued to drive headline volatility,

surging in September, while detached dwellings, which
provide a better read on the underlying trend, recorded a
sizeable decline.
Note that most figures in the charts and table are presented on
a rolling-three month basis to smooth out monthly volatility.
Private detached dwelling approvals fell 5.3%mth in
September, unwinding August’s upwardly-revised 5.4%mth
rise (+1.7ppts). Recent resilience has kept the segment
elevated, with approvals remaining above 10,000 and the

annual pace at 9.6%yr. However, the quarterly profile provides
a clearer read on the underlying trend and suggests detached
dwelling approvals are beginning to respond to recent interest
rate increases (see here). Approvals rose just 0.9%qtr in
September vs 3.1%qtr in June and 5.9%qtr in March.
Private unit approvals remained volatile, rising 16.6%mth in
September following 21.7%mth decline in August. Strength
was led by high-rise approvals, while low-mid-rise approvals

recorded a more modest increase. Despite the monthly rise,
the annual pace fell further into contraction at –20.5%yr.
Among the states, NSW recorded the strongest result,
with private approvals rising 35.3%mth led by a surge in
private unit approvals. Queensland also recorded an increase
(+9.3%mth), while WA (–2.9%mth), SA (–3.5%mth) and
Victoria (–29.0%mth) all declined.
In value terms, residential building approvals fell 2.8%mth,
driven by a 3.3%mth decline in new residential approvals,

partially offset by a 0.8%mth rise in renovation activity.
Non-residential approvals once again reversed sharply, rising
45.9%mth, as the data centre-dominated ‘other commercial’
segment recorded an 8-fold increase on a non-seasonally
adjusted basis. The total value of building approvals rose
13.0%mth in September. On a non-seasonally adjusted basis,
the value of building approvals rose 8.3%mth overall, but
declined 3.0%mth when excluding the ‘other commercial’

segment.
September’s result reinforces the importance of looking
through headline volatility in unit approvals. While total
approvals rose in the month, detached dwelling approvals
slowed on a quarterly basis and fell across all states, pointing
to softer underlying momentum. The annual pace of total
approvals also moved into contraction for the first time
since January. Interest rate increases, the sharp downturn
in established housing markets and rising costs are likely to

weigh on further growth, with pessimistic consumer sentiment
an additional headwind (see here).
Detached approvals point to softer momentum

## Document 311: Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-05)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-05).pdf
Report source: Merrill Lynch (CMO)
Date: 2026-10-05
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Stronger Growth, Costlier Money
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
The latest GDP report and annual revisions show a sturdier U.S. economy than previously
reported. According to the Bureau of Economic Analysis (BEA), real GDP grew at a 2.2%
annualized pace in Q2 compared to 1.5% previously estimated, and Q1 was revised
from 2.1% to 2.5%. Both 2024 and 2025 were also revised slightly higher. Most of the

first half of the year revision was in categories related to the Artificial Intelligence (AI)
buildout, but real consumer spending has also been revised slightly higher, accelerating
from 0.7% annualized growth in Q1 to 3.8% in Q2 despite surging energy prices and
slow hiring.
Spending in July and August remained much stronger than labor market conditions alone
would suggest, also averaging 3.7% annualized growth. Upside revisions to net interest

income and personal saving suggest households have benefited from more income than
was previously apparent, with personal saving revised higher back to 2023. The new data,
according to the BEA, show the January to July 2026 savings rate averaged 4.8% versus
3.3% prerevision, strengthening consumer spending underpinnings despite moderate
hiring, high energy prices and rising borrowing costs.
The BEA update also included inflation methodological changes related to categories

inflated by the AI boom—such as portfolio management fees and computer software and
accessories. These improved the inflation picture to some extent—August “core” PCE
inflation was reduced by 0.3 ppts to 3% YoY, bringing it closer to “core” consumer price
index inflation, which was 2.5% YoY and 2% on a three-month annualized basis. While still
above the 2% Federal Reserve (Fed) target, “core” PCE now appears stable at 3% year over
year since January rather than accelerating. These methodological changes are expected

to dampen pass-through of AI-related price pressures into “core” inflation and effects of
stock-market swings on inflation.
They also produced an encouraging improvement in recent PCE inflation momentum.
Three-month annualized “core” PCE inflation slowed from 4.3% at the peak of tariff
effects in February to 2.1% in August. For durable goods, this rate was down from 5.8%
in February to 1.5% in August, and, for services ex-energy and healthcare, it slowed from

4.2% to 2.2%, for example. Inflation is volatile on a three-month change basis, so it
remains to be seen whether this moderation continues without further Fed tightening.
For now, the trends in the San Francisco Fed’s cyclical versus acyclical inflation measures
diverged again in August. As expected, given softening wage growth, prices most sensitive
to the labor market have eased further. At 3% YoY, this inflation category is down from
8% in early 2023 and 4.6% two years ago is back to prepandemic levels and is likely

to ease more (Exhibit 1A). In contrast, supply bottlenecks, high energy prices and other
industry-specific factors have kept acyclical inflation on an uptrend. As shown in Exhibit
1B, the relentless increase in freight costs could prolong upside pressure on this category.
The diesel market has tightened in recent months, boosting its price by 70% YoY,
consistent with Brent oil at around $200 per barrel and large reverberations across
a wide swath of goods and services prices. A government-policy-related decline in

available truck drivers is also raising transportation costs more than expected. So are
tight ocean shipping capacity and longer/more expensive voyages due to the U.S./Iran
War. Supply-chain stress also increased as companies rushed to front-run potential new
tariff increases.
On the positive side for the inflation outlook, unit labor costs (ULC) are up modestly
as robust productivity growth has absorbed much of the wage growth, limiting a major
source of domestic inflation. Upside productivity growth revisions are likely to further

reduce ULC, which could help shift focus from energy and AI-related inflation to
eventual disinflationary effects once oil and refined fuel prices start to ease. Sustained
productivity gains would support growth and margins while limiting inflation and the
need for much higher interest rates.
Investment Implications
We favor selective Equity exposure
to companies with durable demand
and pricing power, while remaining
cautious about businesses most

exposed to fuel, freight, and
financing costs. We would also
gradually increase long-duration
exposure.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
Surging nominal growth has boosted corporate revenues (up 13% YoY in Q2, according to the
BEA) while contained unit labor costs helped raise margins and profits more than expected.
With labor costs declining as a share of revenues, investment-related tax incentives and
rock-bottom net interest expense, pre-tax nonfinancial corporate profits as measured in GDP
accounts rose 21% YoY in Q2. Total corporate profits after-tax were up 18.2% YoY, according to

the BEA.
Surging AI-related imports and investment are keeping the business and profits cycle on
an uptrend both here and abroad. According to BofA Global Research, the global economic
cycle backdrop improved further in September, as reflected in a broad-based increase in its
Global Wave1 indicator. Broadening U.S. manufacturing strength, fueled by ongoing AI-driven
investment, a healthier orders backlog and low inventories suggest that the expansion has

room to run—a constructive environment for earnings. Typically, a Global Wave upswing is
consistent with rising earnings revisions ratios and equity price uptrends. According to the
Global Wave, global earnings growth expectations remain exceptionally strong.
Still, absent a resolution of war-related energy and transportation disruptions, the same
industrial strength intensifies inflation pressures. A prolonged supply shock raises the risk of
restrictive interest rates and weakening economic growth. In this context, the surge in the

10-year Treasury yield above 5% is starting to reflect more than optimism about U.S. growth
and strengthening productivity outlook. Oil’s tightest recent correlation with 10-year Treasury
yields in 35 years suggests a common energy-price thread behind rising yields in developed
countries.
Bond market spillovers from normalizing Japan yields and capital repatriation are another
common thread. Higher yields on foreign sovereign bonds such as in Japan—which has

escaped stagnation and deflation by intensely pursuing investment-led growth—weaken the
relative appeal of Treasurys. That distinction matters for portfolios. A yield increase driven
mainly by durable productivity gains would come with a stronger outlook for future income
and earnings. A yield increase driven by cost shocks and greater uncertainty raises financing
costs without the same benefit.
In sum, the economy remains more resilient than expected, but the bond market is also

charging more for an uncertain inflation outlook given untethered government debt and
cooling global demand for Treasurys spurred by geopolitical tensions and Japan capital
repatriation. The case for much further tightening becomes stronger if wage growth
strengthens, productivity lags, and unit labor costs reaccelerate, causing cyclical inflation to
also pick up steam. The case becomes weaker if that measure stays contained while fuel and
freight pressures recede or rising Treasury yields reduce asset prices and economic growth.

etc.).
B) Surge in U.S. AI-related imports, tariff front-running, shipping disruptions and fuel- price
spike feed acyclical inflation.

### Page 4

4 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
Yields, Yen and Iran: What Haunts the “House”
Lauren Sanfilippo, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
We recently highlighted the three-front challenge facing Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent:
engineering a stronger yen, containing the 10-year Treasury yield and securing a diplomatic
resolution to the conflict with Iran.

Bessent recently has delivered one of the year’s more memorable lines while discussing
Treasury policy and the yen: “I have asymmetric information. I am the house now.” 2 Yet several
challenges remain: the 10-year Treasury yield is near levels last seen in the early 2000s, the
yen remains structurally weaker, and a phased agreement to reopen the Strait of Hormuz
appears remote. The resulting strain on energy markets keeps inflation risks elevated.

Put differently, oil prices, Treasury yields and the dollar-yen exchange rate are all trending
against Bessent’s objectives. Below, we examine how each variable is haunting his “house.”
Haunting the “House” is the Yen. Since the U.S. and Japan mounted a record $98.7B
coordinated intervention in late July after the yen weakened to a 40-year low near 164 per
dollar, the currency has appreciated roughly 4% against the dollar. The move was further
supported on September 18 when the BoJ raised rates by 25 basis points (bps) to 1.25%,

their highest level since the mid-1990s (Exhibit 2A). However, the split 7–2 vote and Governor
Ueda’s limited forward guidance leave the path of further tightening uncertain. The key
question is whether additional BoJ hikes can produce sustained appreciation in the yen, or
merely reinforce the temporary relief provided by the summer intervention.
Bessent, meanwhile, has repeatedly signaled his preference for a stronger yen. Partly because
yen stability is also a U.S. financing concern. Persistent currency weakness could prompt

Japan to support its currency by selling foreign assets, including U.S. Treasurys—which would
be an unwelcome prospect as Washington partially relies on foreign capital flows to fund its
large fiscal deficits. That potential “haunts” the Treasury Secretary.  At precisely the moment
Washington needs foreign capital to finance its enormous fiscal deficits, it would be inopportune
for one of America’s largest creditors to become a significant seller of Treasury securities.

Haunting the “House” are Yields. The surge in yields runs counter to a White House intent
on lowering borrowing costs. The benchmark 10-year yield has broken above the 5% threshold
and climbed to levels not seen since 2007. At around 5.30%, it stands more than a full
percentage point above where it began the year (Exhibit 2B). The level is striking but so is the
speed of the move: Stronger-than-expected economic data, including a survey showing U.S.

2 September 8, 2026.

complicating the macro backdrop
(i.e., higher-for-longer yields/oil)
the combination of strong earnings
growth, robust consumer demand
(real final sales to private domestic
purchasers grew at 4.6% for Q2) and
ongoing capital investment suggests
that the fundamental backdrop for
U.S. Equities remains constructive.

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
business activity expanded at the fastest pace in more than five years, helped trigger an 18
bps surge in the 10-year yield—among its largest daily moves in decades. The 5-year yield also
topped 5% for the first time since 2007, while the 30-year yield reached 5.63%, its highest
level since 2002.
Compounding the move is inflation, running above the Fed’s 2% target for 67 months,

large fiscal deficits and the prospect of continued growth in Treasury issuance. Against this
backdrop, Treasury bought back $4.1 billion of long-dated debt, versus a maximum of $6
billion. That effort has done little to arrest the selloff. A subsequent auction priced nearly $4
billion of 7-year notes at 5.085%, the highest yield for that maturity in 33 years.
All in, the MOVE index, a key gauge of Treasury market volatility, jumped from 78 to 95 on

Wednesday September 23 and then to 104 the following day, its highest level since the early
days of the Iran war (Exhibit 3A).
Haunting the “House” is the Iran conflict. Early expectations that military pressure and
economic sanctions would quickly bring Iran to the negotiating table have yet to materialize.
Iranian officials have signaled that a full reopening of the Strait of Hormuz remains unlikely
while sanctions stay in place. Although talks between Washington and Tehran are continuing,

conflicting signals and lingering skepticism from both sides have kept tensions elevated.
As a result, oil prices have remained elevated in a range even as some Middle East supply
disruptions began to ease.
Startling is that Brent crude is up more than 60% this year, but refined product prices
have risen even faster. Ongoing disruptions to energy flows through the Strait of Hormuz,
combined with Ukrainian strikes on Russian refining infrastructure have tightened global

supplies of crude oil and refined fuels. Saudi Arabia has restored some drone-disrupted flows,
providing roughly 3.5 million barrels a day of relief (roughly 3% of global consumption).
The squeeze is mostly acute in diesel markets, where inventories remain constrained and
prices continue to climb (Exhibit 3B). U.S. diesel prices recently surpassed $6.50 per gallon,
the highest level on record, just as Russia has extended restrictions on diesel exports into

October. Against this backdrop, proposals to ban U.S. fuel exports risks worsening the
imbalance. According to S&P Global, an export ban could reduce refinery runs by nearly 2
million barrels per day and significantly cut gasoline production, potentially driving gasoline
and jet fuel prices even higher. As long as tensions persist, the oil impulse remains a key
impediment to structurally lower inflation.
Investment implications. Treasury Secretary Bessent increasingly sits at the intersection of

monetary policy, fiscal policy, foreign policy and national security. We remain constructive on
the U.S. economy and overweight U.S. Equities but are watching as these fronts challenge the
narrative. There is a great deal riding on the “house” versus the key macro variables of today.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
When It Comes to Attracting Foreign Capital, the U.S. Remains Exceptional
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
There may be no better vote of confidence in a country than the willingness of
foreigners to put their money there. And by that measurement, the U.S. remains the
exception—both portfolio and FDI inflows are currently running at record highs based

on the latest data. This dynamic is one key reason why we remain constructive on U.S.
assets over the longer term.
Notwithstanding all the handwringing about America’s fiscal deficits, tariffs and rising
government debt, global investors sunk some $942B in net purchases of U.S. Equities in
the 12 months to July, the highest rolling 12-month total on record. In Q2, net purchase
of U.S. Equities soared to $426B, up 62% from the same period a year ago (Exhibit 4A).

By country, flows were down from China—not surprisingly—but strong from Taiwan and
South Korea, who have taken to recycling their massive AI-generated trade surplus back
into the U.S. In the end, strong portfolio inflows suggest that international investors
remain willing to increase their exposure to the world's largest capital market—that’s
bullish for U.S. assets.
Meanwhile, Exhibit 4B highlights the growing push among foreign multinationals to be

inside the U.S., with FDI inflows totaling nearly $240B in the first six months of this year.
That is a staggering 103% jump from a year ago and means FDI inflows are on track for
a record year, estimated at $480B for 2026.
There are multiple factors behind surging inflows: One, due to rising U.S. trade
protectionism, foreign multinationals increasingly want to be “inside” the U.S. economy,
not outside. Two, within the $32 trillion-plus economy with extraordinarily consumer

purchasing power, foreign firms prefer to be closer to their customers via investment
than just trade (exports). Three, America’s massive investment cycle in AI, data centers,
semiconductors, power generation, etc., has created numerous potential investment
opportunities for many foreign firms that don’t want to miss out on America’s surging
capex cycle. Four, and finally, abundant energy supplies, deep capital markets and a highly
developed technological ecosystem have also induced more FDI inflows.

In the end, double-barreled capital inflows speak to the absolute and relative
attractiveness of the U.S. economy and capital markets. Stay invested in America—the
rest of the world is.

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

ICE BofA MOVE Index is a market-implied measure of U.S. bond market volatility.
Consumer Price Index measures the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a market basket of consumer goods and services.
Baltic Dry Index is a daily shipping and trade index published by the Baltic Exchange in London that measures the cost of moving raw materials like iron ore, coal, and grain by sea.
Volatility Index (VIX) is a real-time market indicator that measures the stock market's expected volatility over the next 30 days.

or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and

of America, Merrill or any affiliate to sell or provide, or a specific invitation for a consumer to apply for, any particular retail financial product or service that may be available.

oversight and data analytics. CIO viewpoints are developed for Bank of America Private Bank, a division of Bank of America, N.A., (“Bank of America”) and Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith

The Global Wealth & Investment Management Investment Strategy Committee (“GWIM ISC”) is responsible for developing and coordinating recommendations for short-term and long-term
investment strategy and market views encompassing markets, economic indicators, asset classes and other market-related projections affecting GWIM.

Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

## Document 344: ESG与可持续发展周报：企业可持续披露体系持续完善，进一步健全气候准则配套应用体系

Source PDF: all report pdf/ESG与可持续发展周报：企业可持续披露体系持续完善，进一步健全气候准则配套应用体系.pdf

企业可持续披露体系持续完善，进一步健全气

——ESG 与可持续发展周报（20261026-1101）
[Table_Summary] 主要观点
 政策解读
2026 年 9 月 28 日 ，财 政部 发 布《 〈企 业 可持 续 披露 准则 第 1
号——气候（试行）〉应用指南（征求意见稿）》及电力、钢铁、煤
炭、石油、化肥、铝、氢、水泥、汽车等 9 项行业应用指南（征求意
见稿），意见反馈截止日期为 2026 年 10 月 26 日。
2024 年 11 月，财政部会同外交部、国家发改委、工业和信息化
部、生态环境部、商务部、中国人民银行、国务院国资委、金融监管
总局、中国证监会（以下简称 9 部门）制定了《企业可持续披露准
则——基本准则（试行）》（以下简称基本准则），拉开了国家统一
的可持续披露准则体系建设的序幕。2025 年 12 月，财政部会同 9 部
门制定了气候准则，标志着国家统一的可持续披露准则体系建设从宏

观框架向具体议题纵深推进。
国家统一的可持续披露准则体系由基本准则、具体准则和应用指
南构成。准则应用指南和行业应用指南相互衔接、各有侧重，共同为
企业贯彻实施气候准则提供配套指引。其中，准则应用指南主要聚焦
企业执行气候准则过程种的共性问题，负责对气候准则部分条款进行
解释说明；行业应用指南主要聚焦行业特定事项，侧重于行业特定风
险，机遇和排放源识别，以及指标和目标设定。
准则应用指南共五章。第一章关于气候相关影响信息的披露。对
气候准则中气候相关影响信息的披露流程进行说明，给出识别、分
析、确认和披露四个步骤的具体操作方法。第二章为关于气候相关战
略信息的披露。对气候准则战略方面的披露要求进行细化和示例，包
括气候相关风险和机遇的识别与披露、风险和机遇对企业战略和决策
影响的披露要求、当期和预期财务影响的分析步骤、气候韧性评估等
方面的技术指引。第三章关于气候相关风险和机遇管理信息的披露。
对气候准则风险和机遇管理方面的披露要求进行细化和示例，包括风
险管理流程、机遇管理流程、风险和机遇管理流程融入企业整体风险

管理流程的融合程度与融合路径。第四章为关于温室气体排放信息的
披露。对气候准则中行业通用指标“温室气体排放”的分类、核算流
程和核算方法进行介绍。第五章为关于行业特定指标的披露。对气候
准则中行业特定指标的披露要求进行说明，与行业应用指南实现衔
接。
各行业应用指南在框架上保持一致。第一章为行业介绍。明确各
项行业应用指南的适用范围，并结合行业价值链特征以及在应对气候
变化中面临的实际场景，阐述行业气候相关信息披露的背景。第二章
为行业特定气候相关信息。梳理行业特定的气候相关风险和机遇，以
及企业活动对经济、环境和社会产生的气候相关影响。第三章为行业
特定气候相关风险和机遇的财务影响。针对各行业识别的重点气候相
关风险和机遇，列示其对财务状况、经营成果和现金流量的当期和预
期财务影响。第四章为行业温室气体排放。结合各行业生产工艺和排
放特征，列举各行业常见温室气体排放源。第五章为行业特定指标和
目标。结合各行业特点与实际，设置行业特定指标和目标。
2025 年 12 月，财政部会计司有关负责人就印发《企业可持续披

### Page 2

ESG 与可持续发展周报
露准则 第 1 号——气候（试行）》答记者问时表示：综合考虑我国企
业的发展阶段和披露能力，将坚持准则制定和实施分开的原则，实事
求是、稳中求进，沿用《基本准则》的实施安排，不采取“一刀切”
的强制实施要求，现阶段将《气候准则》定位为试行文件，明确在实
施范围和实施要求作出规定之前，由企业自愿实施。后续将采取区分
重点、试点先行、循序渐进、分步推进的策略，从上市公司向非上市
公司扩展，从大型企业向中小企业扩展，从定性要求向定量要求扩
展，从自愿披露向强制披露扩展。
我们认为，指南的制定发布，标志着气候准则配套应用体系进一
步健全，对规范我国企业气候相关信息披露、引导企业践行可持续发
展理念、完善国家统一的可持续披露准则体系具有重要意义。强化了
支持绿色低碳发展的标准供给，有助于引导市场预期、规范企业行
为、科学评估转型进展，为“双碳”目标从国家宏观战略向企业微观
行动转化提供了关键的政策工具与制度基础设施。
 行业动态
（1）自然资源部 28 日发布消息，前三季度，我国海洋经济总体保持

平稳，发展态势向新向优，发展韧性持续彰显。前三季度，海洋经济
总量保持平稳增长。初步核算，前三季度我国海洋生产总值 8.5 万亿
元，同比增长 4.9%，高于国内生产总值增速 0.3 个百分点。海洋经济
引擎作用持续释放。作为国民经济重要增长极，海洋经济拉动国民经
济增长 0.4 个百分点。海洋资源要素保障机制不断完善。海域资源要
素配置改革加快向纵深推进，积极探索海域分层立体利用，优化提升
海域资源管理水平。
（2）近日，交通运输部等八部门联合印发《综合交通枢纽发展“十五
五”规划》（简称《规划》），聚焦提升国内外、干支线、跨方式衔
接转换效率，着力提能级、强连接、增质效，统筹推进综合交通枢纽
集群、枢纽城市、枢纽港站建设，打造旅客便捷换乘中心和物流高效
集散中心。《规划》将安全、智慧、绿色理念贯穿枢纽建设运营，要
求提高综合客运枢纽安全应急能力，提升综合货运枢纽安全韧性；完
善综合客运枢纽电子客票服务，加强不同运输方式票务系统的有效衔
接，推进综合货运枢纽仓储、运输、配送等设施设备智能化和自动化

升级；依托国家综合交通运输信息平台，推动各种运输方式信息共
享，提高枢纽港站数据资源的采集与汇聚能力；推动综合客运枢纽绿
色化转型，打造零碳综合货运枢纽，推动新能源重卡在枢纽应用，推
动物流设施设备“油改电”。
 技术前沿
近日，清华大学碳中和研究院工业深度减碳研究中心双聘教师、
清华大学化工系宝丰碳中和讲席教授唐军旺团队在甲醇绿色转化领域
取得新进展。团队提出光子-声子协同驱动催化策略，通过 Li 调控 Pt
单原子局域结构，实现了温和条件下甲醇高选择性制甲醛并副产氢
气。
该研究将体相光物理过程与表面化学反应相衔接，通过光子与声
子 分 别 满 足 不 同 的 反 应 步 骤 所 需 的 能 量 （ 也 即 能 量 的 量 化 供 应 概
念），为克服单一能量场下的效率与选择性限制提供了新思路，也为
低温甲醛绿色合成及联产绿氢提供了工业放大基础。研究成果以“甲
醇绿色合成氢气与甲醛的量子效率增强研究”（Green synthesis of
hydrogen and formaldehyde from methanol with enhanced quantum

efficiency ） 为 题 ， 于 10 月 5 日 发 表 于 《 自 然 · 可 持 续 发 展 》
（Nature Sustainability）。

### Page 3

ESG 与可持续发展周报
 研究探索
2026 年 10 月 21 日，在 2026 金融街论坛年会“融智·融绿·融
新——金融创新构建未来可持续投资新生态”分论坛上，中国社会科
学院金融研究所研究员、国家金融与发展实验室理事张跃文发布《中
国金融创新与可持续投资发展报告》。当前资本大量涌向人工智能等
短期商业回报显著的赛道，而周期长、规模大、收益不确定的可持续
投资领域却存在大量的资金缺口，在目前这个阶段，这两类投资一定
程度上形成了资本争夺。
报告显示，我国可持续投资的总体发展态势较好，广义责任投资
规模已经超过了 50 万亿元，其中绿色信贷大概占 90%的比重，与此
同时绿色产业基金、ESG 股权基金、绿色债券、绿色 REITs 等工具不
断丰富，已经初步形成了覆盖多种工具的多层次可持续投资的市场体
系，绿色金融和科技金融的协同效益开始显现，为未来产业注入动
能。但基于周期长、收益滞后；不确定性高；价值维度多元化，社会
效益与经济效益不完全同步；不同阶段的融资需求差异大；信息识别

复杂这五大特征。当前可持续投资也相应暴露出五大薄弱环节：长期
资本供给不足，保险等长期资金对项目投资收益和现金流确定性有较
高要求；缺乏适配项目的个性化金融产品；行业缺少成熟的估值定价
体系；各类金融工具之间缺少平滑衔接的过渡产品；同时专业人才缺
口大，懂绿色环保的不一定懂金融，懂金融的对绿色环保的认识可能
又不够深入。报告研究通过金融创新去解决目前可持续投资所存在的
问题，但这个金融创新不仅包括金融产品和服务的创新，也包括金融
运行模式、金融生态、金融监管规则的创新。要围绕估值定价、信息
识别、期限匹配、风险分担、资产盘活五大作用机制发力。
 国际合作
2026 年 10 月 26 日，“一带一路”绿色创新大会在香港举办。会
议以“携手南方绿色转型，共促全球可持续发展”为主题，由生态环
境部与香港特别行政区政府环境及生态局共同主办，中心与“一带一
路”绿色发展国际联盟（以下简称绿色联盟）、香港特别行政区政府
环境保护署共同承办。生态环境部部长黄润秋、香港特别行政区行政
长官李家超出席开幕式并致辞，来自 20 余个国家及地区和国际组织的

近 300 名代表参会。会议期间，中心主任、绿色联盟副理事长张志锋
主持“专题对话二：共促绿色低碳创新技术与产业协同”，与共建
“一带一路”国家环境主管部门、国际组织和科研机构代表，围绕全
球南方绿色低碳创新技术与产业合作进行研讨。
 投资建议
我们认为，《〈企业可持续披露准则第 1 号——气候（试行）〉
应用指南（征求意见稿）》的制定发布，标志着气候准则配套应用体
系进一步健全，对规范我国企业气候相关信息披露、引导企业践行可
持续发展理念、完善国家统一的可持续披露准则体系具有重要意义。
强化了支持绿色低碳发展的标准供给，有助于引导市场预期、规范企
业行为、科学评估转型进展，为“双碳”目标从国家宏观战略向企业
微观行动转化提供了关键的政策工具与制度基础设施。
 风险提示
ESG相关政策推行不及预期；市场情绪与偏好波动风险等。

### Page 1

Muted iPhone 18 Pro Cycle and Rising Memory Costs

[Table_Rank]

Maintained  Hold  
 Target price US$334

[Table_Summary] Lower TP to $334, Hold rating: Driven by investors being away from AI themes, Apple share 
price outperformed Nasdaq by 7.2% in the past one month. However, we continue to expect 
muted cycle of iPhone 18 P/PM with constrained volume of iPhone Duo. Additionally, memory 
cost hike will last longer than expected, driven by our view of tight DRAM/NAND supply, and 
we expect cost hikes throughout CY2027. For hardware, we still expect the iPhone 20 (2H27) 
to be a strong product cycle, thanks to new design of display, followed by Apple’s early move 
to TSMC ’s A14 in 2H28 (suggesting only 2 -years at 2nm). Additionally, Apple Intelligence 
remains the key to watch. Yet, the share price trades at 35x FY27E P/E, suggesting the stock 
isn’t cheap. By factoring in results, guidance and lower shipments/margins, we revise FY27E 
EPS by -10% and revise TP from $369 to $334, still based on 35x FY27E. 
Dec-Q margin guidance below expect ations: Apple delivered solid Sep t-Q results, with 
revenue of $114.5bn (+12% YoY) and diluted EPS of $2.1 (+14% YoY), both ahead of 
consensus. For Dec-Q, mgmt. guided to total revenue growth of 7 –10% YoY (vs. consensus 
of +6.5%), Services growth of 8 –9%, and gross margin of 45 –46%. The revenue outlook is 
constructive to Street expectations, though the margin guidance and Services deceleration 
were disappointing, reflecting cost hikes and mix headwinds.  
The iPhone product cycle mixed, with challenges in FY2027: Mgmt sees a strong start of 
iPhone Duo but remains supply constrained. Hence, we continue to expect the EMS build plan 
of 6.5m of iPhone Duo by end-2026 with limited upside, while the mainstream iPhone 18 P/PM 
to be 74.5m. As said in our earlier report of iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max Lukewarm on Sept 15, we 
believe the muted demand will weigh on CY1H27 EMS builds. Margin wise, despite p ricing 
actions taken earlier, margin will likely be under pressure, as the CEO Ternus characterized 
current memory pricing as “unprecedented,” with higher costs in December and beyond.  
Apple positioning the rebuilt Siri AI as a core differentiator: The CEO Ternus emphasized 
that the new Siri is deeply integrated and privacy -preserving and is fielding ~2.5bn requests 
per day. From a financial perspective, CFO Parekh noted that these Si ri AI costs are 
embedded in the December margin outlook, suggesting near-term gross-margin compression 
alongside the memory cost up-cycle.  
Risks: 1) weak demand, 2) competition, 3) product delays, 4) geo-tension.

### Page 2

Earnings Forecast  
Solid September Quarter Results  
Apple delivered a solid September-quarter, with revenue of $114.5bn (+12% YoY) and diluted EPS of 
$2.10 (+14% YoY), both ahead of consensus, reaching a record fiscal year of $479bn in revenue. Gross 
margin came in at 47.9%, aided by approximately one percentage point of tariff refund benefits (and a 
$0.05 EPS tailwind).  
iPhone revenue reached a September-quarter record of $57.6bn (+17.5% YoY) likely driven by higher 
ASPs, Mac rose a strong 24% to $10.8bn likely on edge-AI, Services set at $31.6bn (+9.8% YoY) which 
was below expectations, while iPad declined 10% to $6.2bn and Wearables fell 8% to $8.3bn. The 
company returned $28bn to shareholders. 
December Quarter Guidance Slightly Ahead in Revenue but Shy in Margin  
For the December quarter, mgmt. guided to total revenue growth of 7 –10% YoY ( vs. consensus of 
+6.5%), Services growth of 8–9%, and gross margin of 45–46%. Operating expenses are expected at 
$20.4–20.8bn, OI&E around $300 million, and the tax rate near 17%. The revenue outlook is 
constructive relative to prior Street expectations, though the margin range and Services deceleration 
reflect known cost and mix headwinds.

Valuation and Recommendation  
Hold with TP of $334 
We rate Apple (AAPL US) Hold and set our TP at $334 which is based on 35x our FY27E EPS estimate. 
We believe it is important to capture the 2H26 -3Q27’s iPhone 18 product cycle, given high market 
interest. We adopt P/E methodology as we believe the market now mainly focuses on the company’s 
earnings outlook, and we usually apply P/E method to downstream firms.  
We believe Apple’s ecosystem will remain strong and associated revenue durability is high. This is 
backed by Apple's installed base growth, double digit YoY growth in services. However, the market 
would wait for visibility for its Apple Intelligence. Meanwhile, the market will need more visibility on the 
higher-high retail prices for the iPhone 18 series due to the rising cost over tariff and 2nm/memories. 
That said, we expect its earnings growth to be  high-teens to 20% YoY in FY26E-FY28E, from single 
digit YoY in FY22-FY24. Considering >30x one-year P/E, the stock doesn’t look particularly cheap.

## Document 446: Top of Mind_ Higher bond yields_ how concerning_

Source PDF: all report pdf/Top of Mind_ Higher bond yields_ how concerning_.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-10-13
Pages: 31
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The Goldman Sachs Group, Inc.
Global bond yields have risen to multi-decade highs, yet growth and markets have
remained remarkably resilient. But with some pressures beginning to emerge (e.g. in
European sovereign bond markets), can this resilience last, or will more vulnerabilities
be revealed? We ask the IMF’s Tobias Adrian, Moody’s Analytics’ Mark Zandi, and GS
economists. Adrian is relatively sanguine about the economy’s resilience, though
notes downside risks, while GS economists expect only a modest growth drag from

higher rates, but a more consequential one if yields remain near current or higher
levels. Zandi is generally more concerned, describing the US economy as vulnerable
rather than resilient, and sees one-in-three odds of the economy going off the rails.
Meanwhile, GS strategists and analysts are not especially concerned about banks or
markets more broadly, but warn that further increases in yields or rate volatility could
test risk assets. Key to watch: whether the increasingly interconnected and leveraged

AI investment boom can continue to withstand higher rates.
Tobias Adrian, Special Advisor and former Director of Monetary and Capital Markets, IMF
“The global economy has demonstrated considerable resilience in the face of multiple severe shocks, and I would not be
surprised if it continues to shrug off higher yields, provided they do not keep rising sharply in a disorderly fashion. At the same
time, I do worry about downside risks.”
Mark Zandi, Chief Economist, Moody’s Analytics
